Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The martingales variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 974 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 48 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the martingales space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 974 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 48. QED.
